)
we get

\displaystyle c^{[1,8_{a}]}

\displaystyle=\frac{1}{(N_{c}^{2}-1)\sqrt{N_{c}}}\,\delta^{a_{5}a_{1}}\,if^{a_{3}a_{4}a_{2}}\,,

\displaystyle c^{[8_{a},1]}

\displaystyle=\frac{1}{(N_{c}^{2}-1)\sqrt{N_{c}}}\,\delta^{a_{3}a_{2}}\,if^{a_{1}a_{5}a_{4}}\,,

\displaystyle c^{[8_{s},8_{s}]}

\displaystyle=\frac{\sqrt{N_{c}}}{(N_{c}^{2}-4)\sqrt{N_{c}^{2}-1}}\,d^{a_{1}a_{5}x}\,if^{ya_{4}x}\,d^{a_{2}a_{3}y}\,,

\displaystyle c^{[8_{s},8_{a}]}

\displaystyle=\frac{\sqrt{N_{c}}}{(N_{c}^{2}-4)\sqrt{N_{c}^{2}-1}}\,d^{a_{1}a_{5}x}\,d^{ya_{4}x}\,if^{a_{2}a_{3}y}\,,

\displaystyle c^{[8_{a},8_{s}]}

\displaystyle=\frac{\sqrt{N_{c}}}{(N_{c}^{2}-4)\sqrt{N_{c}^{2}-1}}\,if^{a_{1}a_{5}x}\,d^{ya_{4}x}\,d^{a_{2}a_{3}y}\,,

\displaystyle c^{[8_{a},8_{a}]}

\displaystyle=\frac{1}{\sqrt{N_{c}^{3}(N_{c}^{2}-1)}}\,if^{a_{1}a_{5}x}\,if^{ya_{4}x}\,if^{a_{2}a_{3}y}\,,

\displaystyle c^{[8_{s},10-\overline{10}]}

\displaystyle=\frac{\sqrt{2N_{c}}}{(N_{c}^{2}-4)\sqrt{N_{c}^{2}-1}}\,d^{a_{1}a_{5}x}\,P_{\mathbf{10}-\mathbf{\overline{10}}}^{a_{3}a_{2}xa_{4}}\,,

\displaystyle c^{[10-\overline{10},8_{s}]}

\displaystyle=\frac{\sqrt{2N_{c}}}{(N_{c}^{2}-4)\sqrt{N_{c}^{2}-1}}\,d^{a_{2}a_{3}x}\,P_{\mathbf{10}-\mathbf{\overline{10}}}^{a_{1}a_{5}a_{4}x}\,,

\displaystyle c^{[8_{a},10+\overline{10}]}

\displaystyle=\frac{\sqrt{2}}{\sqrt{N_{c}(N_{c}^{2}-4)(N_{c}^{2}-1)}}\,if^{a_{1}a_{5}x}\,P_{\mathbf{10}+\mathbf{\overline{10}}}^{a_{3}a_{2}xa_{4}}\,,

\displaystyle c^{[10+\overline{10},8_{a}]}

\displaystyle=\frac{\sqrt{2}}{\sqrt{N_{c}(N_{c}^{2}-4)(N_{c}^{2}-1)}}\,if^{a_{2}a_{3}x}\,P_{\mathbf{10}+\mathbf{\overline{10}}}^{a_{1}a_{5}a_{4}x}\,,

\displaystyle c^{[8_{a},27]}

\displaystyle=\frac{2}{\sqrt{N_{c}^{3}(N_{c}+3)(N_{c}-1)}}\,if^{a_{1}a_{5}x}\,P_{\mathbf{27}}^{a_{3}a_{2}xa_{4}}\,,

(341)

\displaystyle c^{[27,8_{a}]}

\displaystyle=\frac{2}{\sqrt{N_{c}^{3}(N_{c}+3)(N_{c}-1)}}\,if^{a_{2}a_{3}x}\,P_{\mathbf{27}}^{a_{1}a_{5}a_{4}x}\,,

\displaystyle c^{[8_{a},0]}

\displaystyle=\frac{2}{\sqrt{N_{c}^{3}(N_{c}-3)(N_{c}+1)}}\,if^{a_{1}a_{5}x}\,P_{\mathbf{0}}^{a_{3}a_{2}xa_{4}}\,,

\displaystyle c^{[0,8_{a}]}

\displaystyle=\frac{2}{\sqrt{N_{c}^{3}(N_{c}-3)(N_{c}+1)}}\,if^{a_{2}a_{3}x}\,P_{\mathbf{0}}^{a_{1}a_{5}a_{4}x}\,,

\displaystyle c^{[10,\overline{10}]_{1}}

\displaystyle=\frac{2}{\sqrt{N_{c}(N_{c}^{2}-4)(N_{c}^{2}-1)}}\,P_{\mathbf{10}+\mathbf{\overline{10}}}^{a_{1}a_{5}zx}\,if^{ya_{4}x}\,P_{\mathbf{10}+\mathbf{\overline{10}}}^{a_{3}a_{2}yz}\,,

\displaystyle c^{[10,\overline{10}]_{2}}

\displaystyle=\frac{2\sqrt{N_{c}}}{\sqrt{(N_{c}^{2}-4)(N_{c}^{2}-1)(N_{c}+3)(N_{c}-3)}}\,\bigg(P_{\mathbf{10}-\mathbf{\overline{10}}}^{a_{1}a_{5}zx}\,d^{ya_{4}x}\,P_{\mathbf{10}+\mathbf{\overline{10}}}^{a_{3}a_{2}yz}

\displaystyle\hskip 199.16928pt+\,\frac{1}{N_{c}}P_{\mathbf{10}+\mathbf{\overline{10}}}^{a_{1}a_{5}zx}\,if^{ya_{4}x}\,P_{\mathbf{10}+\mathbf{\overline{10}}}^{a_{3}a_{2}yz}\bigg)\,,

\displaystyle c^{[10+\overline{10},27]}

\displaystyle=\frac{2\sqrt{2}}{\sqrt{N_{c}(N_{c}^{2}-1)(N_{c}-2)(N_{c}+3)}}\,P_{\mathbf{10}+\mathbf{\overline{10}}}^{a_{1}a_{5}zx}\,if^{ya_{4}x}\,P_{\mathbf{27}}^{a_{3}a_{2}yz}\,,

\displaystyle c^{[27,10+\overline{10}]}

\displaystyle=\frac{2\sqrt{2}}{\sqrt{N_{c}(N_{c}^{2}-1)(N_{c}-2)(N_{c}+3)}}\,P_{\mathbf{27}}^{a_{1}a_{5}zx}\,if^{ya_{4}x}\,P_{\mathbf{10}+\mathbf{\overline{10}}}^{a_{3}a_{2}yz}\,,

\displaystyle c^{[10+\overline{10},0]}

\displaystyle=\frac{2\sqrt{2}}{\sqrt{N_{c}(N_{c}^{2}-1)(N_{c}+2)(N_{c}-3)}}\,P_{\mathbf{10}+\mathbf{\overline{10}}}^{a_{1}a_{5}zx}\,if^{ya_{4}x}\,P_{\mathbf{0}}^{a_{3}a_{2}yz}\,,

\displaystyle c^{[0,10+\overline{10}]}

\displaystyle=\frac{2\sqrt{2}}{\sqrt{N_{c}(N_{c}^{2}-1)(N_{c}+2)(N_{c}-3)}}\,P_{\mathbf{0}}^{a_{1}a_{5}zx}\,if^{ya_{4}x}\,P_{\mathbf{10}+\mathbf{\overline{10}}}^{a_{3}a_{2}yz}\,,

\displaystyle c^{[27,27]}

\displaystyle=\frac{2\sqrt{2}}{N_{c}\sqrt{(N_{c}^{2}-1)(N_{c}+3)}}\,P_{\mathbf{27}}^{a_{1}a_{5}zx}\,if^{ya_{4}x}\,P_{\mathbf{27}}^{a_{3}a_{2}yz}\,,

\displaystyle c^{[0,0]}

\displaystyle=\frac{2\sqrt{2}}{N_{c}\sqrt{(N_{c}^{2}-1)(N_{c}-3)}}\,P_{\mathbf{0}}^{a_{1}a_{5}zx}\,if^{ya_{4}x}\,P_{\mathbf{0}}^{a_{3}a_{2}yz}\,.

In this basis, the quadratic operators
measuring the colour flow in the
t

channel reads

\displaystyle\mathbf{T}_{t_{1}}^{2}=

\displaystyle\,\text{diag}\big[0,N_{c},N_{c},N_{c},N_{c},N_{c},N_{c},2N_{c},N_{c},2N_{c},N_{c},2(N_{c}+1),N_{c},2(N_{c}-1),

\displaystyle\hskip 56.9055pt2N_{c},2N_{c},2N_{c},2(N_{c}+1),2N_{c},2(N_{c}-1),2(N_{c}+1),2(N_{c}-1)\big]\,,

(342)

and

\displaystyle\mathbf{T}_{t_{2}}^{2}=

\displaystyle\,\text{diag}\big[N_{c},0,N_{c},N_{c},N_{c},N_{c},2N_{c},N_{c},2N_{c},N_{c},2(N_{c}+1),N_{c},2(N_{c}-1),N_{c},

\displaystyle\hskip 56.9055pt2N_{c},2N_{c},2(N_{c}+1),2N_{c},2(N_{c}-1),2N_{c},2(N_{c}+1),2(N_{c}-1)\big]\,.

(343)

B.5
Color space formalism

Given the color basis defined above,
an amplitude is interpreted as a vector
in color space. This is best expressed
by writing the amplitude in vector form

{\cal M}_{ij\to igj}=|{\cal M}_{ij\to igj}\rangle
,

[
72
,
73
,
74
]
,
then one has

{\cal M}_{ij\to igj}=\sum_{i}|c^{[i]}\rangle\langle c^{[i]}|{\cal M}_{ij\to igj}\rangle=\sum_{i}c^{[i]}{\cal M}^{[i]}_{ij\to igj},

(344)

where in the second equality we have implicitly
defined the vector of color basis element

|c^{[i]}\rangle\equiv c^{[i]}
, and the
corresponding coefficients

{\cal M}^{[i]}_{ij\to igj}\equiv\langle c^{[i]}|{\cal M}_{ij\to igj}\rangle
,
as used in the main text, for instance in
eq. (
26
). Within this formalism,
the action of a color operator
\mathbf{T}_{X}
on the
amplitude reads

\mathbf{T}_{X}\,{\cal M}_{ij\to igj}=\sum_{ij}|c^{[j]}\rangle\underbrace{\langle c^{[j]}|\mathbf{T}_{X}|c^{[i]}\rangle\langle c^{[i]}|{\cal M}_{ij\to igj}\rangle}_{\langle c^{[j]}|{\cal M}_{ij\to igj}\rangle}=\sum_{ji}c^{[j]}\underbrace{\mathbf{T}_{X}^{[j][i]}{\cal M}^{[i]}_{ij\to igj}}_{{\cal M}^{[j]}_{ij\to igj}},

(345)

where
\mathbf{T}_{X}^{[j][i]}\equiv\langle c^{[j]}|\mathbf{T}_{X}|c^{[i]}\rangle

represents the matrix in color space
associated with the color operator
\mathbf{T}_{X}
.
In the main text we have used the notation
in the second equality in eq. (
345
),
for instance in eq. (
177
).

The tree level amplitudes for the
processes
qq\to qgq
,
qg\to qgg
,

gg\to ggg
have a single non-zero
element, corresponding to the propagation
of a gluon from the projectile
i
to the
target
j
, with emission of a gluon
g
,
as indicated in eq. (
178
):

\displaystyle{\cal M}_{qq\to qgq}^{\rm tree}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle c^{[8,8]_{a}}\,\big({\cal M}_{qq\to qgq}^{\rm tree}\big)^{[8,8]_{a}},

\displaystyle{\cal M}_{qg\to qgg}^{\rm tree}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle c^{[8,8_{a}]_{a}}\,\big({\cal M}_{qg\to qgg}^{\rm tree}\big)^{[8,8_{a}]_{a}},

(346)

\displaystyle{\cal M}_{gg\to ggg}^{\rm tree}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle c^{[8_{a},8_{a}]}\,\big({\cal M}_{gg\to ggg}^{\rm tree}\big)^{[8_{a},8_{a}]}.

Comparison between eqs. (
22
), (
24
)
and the color bases in eqs. (
325
), (
335
),
(
338
) and (
341
) immediately gives

\displaystyle\big({\cal M}_{qq\to qgq}^{\rm tree}\big)^{[8,8]_{a}}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\frac{\sqrt{N_{c}}\sqrt{N_{c}^{2}-1}}{2}\,C_{i}^{(0)}\,\mathcal{K}^{(0)}\,C_{j}^{(0)},

\displaystyle\big({\cal M}_{qg\to qgg}^{\rm tree}\big)^{[8,8_{a}]_{a}}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\frac{N_{c}\sqrt{\left(N_{c}^{2}-1\right)}}{\sqrt{2}}\,C_{i}^{(0)}\,\mathcal{K}^{(0)}\,C_{j}^{(0)},

(347)

\displaystyle\big({\cal M}_{gg\to ggg}^{\rm tree}\big)^{[8_{a},8_{a}]}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\sqrt{N_{c}^{3}(N_{c}^{2}-1)}\,C_{i}^{(0)}\,\mathcal{K}^{(0)}\,C_{j}^{(0)}.

Appendix C
Variables conversion in the MRK power expansion

We provide here details about the conversion between the minimal set of variables
(w,\bar{w},X_{34},X_{45},|\mathbf{q}_{2}|^{2})
used in section
3.2
and the one used elsewhere,
(z,\bar{z},s_{1},s_{2},r)
. Since
X_{34}
and
X_{45}
are dimensionless, while
s_{1}
,
s_{2}
, and
r
carry mass dimension squared, we need one more variable to normalize by in the conversion, which we pick to be
|\mathbf{q}_{2}|^{2}
(see eq. (
13
)). Nevertheless, since we consider dimensionless functions,
r_{i}/\mathcal{M}^{(0)}
, this additional variable
|\mathbf{q}_{2}|^{2}
never appears in the reconstruction. The relations read,

\displaystyle X_{34}

\displaystyle=\frac{1}{x}\frac{s_{2}w\bar{w}}{|\mathbf{q}_{2}|^{2}}-w-\bar{w}-x\frac{|\mathbf{q}_{2}|^{2}}{s_{2}}

(348)

\displaystyle X_{45}

\displaystyle=\frac{1}{x}\frac{s_{1}w\bar{w}}{|\mathbf{q}_{2}|^{2}(1-w)(1-\bar{w})}+\frac{-2+w+\bar{w}}{(1-w)(1-\bar{w})}-x\frac{|\mathbf{q}_{2}|^{2}}{s_{1}w\bar{w}}

(349)

\displaystyle w

\displaystyle=z+x\,\frac{rz(1-z)}{s_{1}s_{2}(z-\bar{z})}[s_{1}(1-z)+s_{2}z]-x^{2}\,\frac{r^{2}z(1-z)}{s_{1}^{2}s_{2}^{2}(z-\bar{z})^{3}}\times

\displaystyle\qquad\Big[s_{1}^{2}(1-z)^{2}(z^{2}+\bar{z}-z\bar{z}-\bar{z}^{2})+s_{2}^{2}z^{2}(z^{2}+\bar{z}(2-\bar{z})-z(1+\bar{z}))\,\,+

\displaystyle\qquad+s_{1}s_{2}z(1-z)(-z(1-z)+3\bar{z}(1-\bar{z}))\Big]

(350)

where
r=s_{1}s_{2}/s
. Similarly, for
\bar{w}
, through a
z\leftrightarrow\bar{z}
swap of eq. (
350
).

Appendix D
Gluon Regge trajectory, impact factors and vertex

In this appendix we provide the impact factors
at one loop and the Regge trajectory up to two
loops. Furthermore, we discuss their factorization
and renormalization scale dependence, for ease of
comparison with appendix A of
[
23
]
,
where impact factors and Regge trajectory are given
for
\mu^{2}=\tau=-t
.

Les us start by recalling the perturbative
expansions introduced in eqs. (
63
),
(
66
), (
67
),
(
68
),
indicating the full scale dependence: we have

\alpha_{g}(-t,\mu^{2})=\frac{g(\mu^{2})^{2}}{4\pi^{2}}\alpha_{g}^{(1)}(-t,\mu^{2})+\left(\frac{g(\mu^{2})^{2}}{4\pi^{2}}\right)^{2}\alpha_{g}^{(2)}(-t,\mu^{2})+\dots\,,

(351)

for the Regge trajectory, then

C_{i}(-t,\tau,\mu^{2})=g(\mu^{2})\,C_{i}^{(0)}(-t,\tau,\mu^{2})\left[1+\frac{g(\mu^{2})^{2}}{4\pi^{2}}c_{i}^{(1)}(-t,\tau,\mu^{2})+\dots\right]\,,

(352)

for the impact factors, and

\displaystyle V(t_{1},t_{2},|\mathbf{p}_{4}|^{2},\tau,\mu^{2})

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle g(\mu^{2})\,V^{(0)}(t_{1},t_{2},|\mathbf{p}_{4}|^{2},\tau,\mu^{2})

(353)

\displaystyle\times\,\left[1+\frac{g(\mu^{2})^{2}}{4\pi^{2}}v^{(1)}(t_{1},t_{2},|\mathbf{p}_{4}|^{2},\tau,\mu^{2})+\dots\right]\,,

for the Lipatov vertex. The perturbative
coefficients reads respectively

\displaystyle\alpha_{g}^{(1)}(-t,\mu^{2})

\displaystyle=\bigg(\frac{\mu^{2}}{-t}\bigg)^{\epsilon}\frac{r_{\Gamma}}{2\epsilon}=\left(\frac{\mu^{2}}{-t}\right)^{\epsilon}\frac{e^{\epsilon\gamma_{E}}\Gamma^{2}(1-\epsilon)\Gamma(1+\epsilon)}{2\epsilon\,\Gamma(1-2\epsilon)},

(354a)

\displaystyle\alpha_{g}^{(2)}(-t,\mu^{2})

\displaystyle=\left(\frac{\mu^{2}}{-t}\right)^{2\epsilon}\bigg\{-\frac{b_{0}}{16\epsilon^{2}}+\frac{1}{8\epsilon}\left[\left(\frac{67}{18}-\zeta_{2}\right)C_{A}-\frac{10T_{R}n_{f}}{9}\right]

\displaystyle\hskip 71.13188pt+\,C_{A}\left(\frac{101}{108}-\frac{\zeta_{3}}{8}\right)-\frac{7\,T_{R}n_{f}}{27}+{\cal O}(\epsilon)\bigg\}\,,

(354b)

for the Regge trajectory, and
[
131
,
59
]

\displaystyle c_{q}^{(1)}

\displaystyle=\frac{r_{\Gamma}}{4}\left(\frac{\mu^{2}}{-t}\right)^{\epsilon}\frac{1}{\epsilon(1-2\epsilon)}\bigg\{C_{A}\bigg[(1-2\epsilon)\left(\log\frac{\tau}{-t}+\psi(1+\epsilon)-2\psi(-\epsilon)+\psi(1)\right)

\displaystyle\hskip 56.9055pt+\,\frac{1}{4(3-2\epsilon)}+\frac{1}{\epsilon}-\frac{7}{4}\bigg]+\frac{1}{N_{c}}\bigg(\frac{1}{\epsilon}-\frac{1-2\epsilon}{2}\bigg)-n_{f}\frac{1-\epsilon}{3-2\epsilon}\bigg\}-\frac{b_{0}}{8\epsilon}\,,

(355)

\displaystyle c_{g}^{(1)}

\displaystyle=\frac{r_{\Gamma}}{4}\left(\frac{\mu^{2}}{-t}\right)^{\epsilon}\bigg\{C_{A}\bigg[-\frac{2}{\epsilon^{2}}+\frac{1}{\epsilon}\bigg(\log\frac{\tau}{-t}+\psi(1+\epsilon)-2\psi(-\epsilon)+\psi(1)\bigg)

\displaystyle\hskip 56.9055pt+\,\frac{1}{\epsilon(1-2\epsilon)}\left(\frac{1-\epsilon}{2(3-2\epsilon)}-2\right)\bigg]+n_{f}\frac{1-\epsilon}{\epsilon(1-2\epsilon)(3-2\epsilon)}\bigg\}-\frac{b_{0}}{8\epsilon}\,,

(356)

respectively for the quark and gluon impact factor.
Note that in these equations, the parameter
\delta_{R}

related to the regularization scheme has been set to 1,
corresponding to the HV or CDR scheme,
see
[
131
,
59
]
for further
details. In these equations
b_{0}
represents the one
loop coefficient of the beta function: defining

\mu^{2}\frac{d}{d\mu^{2}}a_{s}=-\epsilon\,a_{s}-b_{0}\,a_{s}^{2}-b_{1}\,a_{s}^{3}+\dots\,

(357)

with
a_{s}=g_{s}^{2}/(4\pi)^{2}
, we have

\displaystyle b_{0}

\displaystyle=\frac{11C_{A}-2n_{f}}{3},

(358a)

\displaystyle b_{1}

\displaystyle=\frac{34}{3}C_{A}^{2}-\frac{10}{3}C_{A}n_{f}-2C_{F}n_{f}\,.

(358b)

In order to relate the coefficients
in eqs. (
354
) and (
355
) to the one
in appendix A of
[
23
]
,
given at
\mu^{2}=\tau=-t
, we consider
the factorised expression for the

2\to 2
amplitude

\displaystyle\text{Disp.}\Big\{\mathcal{M}_{ij\to ij}\Big\}

\displaystyle=2\frac{s}{t}\,C_{i}(-t,\tau,\mu^{2})\,\left(\frac{s}{\tau}\right)^{C_{A}\alpha_{g}(-t,\mu^{2})}\,C_{j}(-t,\tau,\mu^{2}).

(359)

Expanding to one-loop order one finds

\displaystyle\text{Disp.}\Big\{\mathcal{M}_{ij\to ij}\Big\}

\displaystyle=2\frac{s}{t}\,g(\mu^{2})^{2}\,C_{i}^{(0)}(-t,\tau,\mu^{2})\,C_{j}^{(0)}(-t,\tau,\mu^{2})\Bigg[1+

\displaystyle\hskip-56.9055pt+\,\frac{g(\mu^{2})^{2}}{4\pi^{2}}\left(c_{i}^{(1)}(-t,\tau,\mu^{2})+c_{j}^{(1)}(-t,\tau,\mu^{2})+C_{A}\alpha_{g}^{(1)}(-t,\mu^{2})\log\frac{s}{\tau}\right)+\dots\Bigg],

(360)

Since the amplitude itself is renormalisation group
invariant, equating the perturbative expansion above
for generic
\mu^{2}
and for
\mu^{2}=-t
:

\displaystyle g(-t)C_{i}^{(0)}(-t,\tau,-t)

\displaystyle+\frac{g(-t)^{3}}{4\pi^{2}}C_{i}^{(0)}(-t,\tau,-t)\,c_{i}^{(1)}(-t,\tau,-t)+\dots

\displaystyle\hskip-56.9055pt=\,g(\mu^{2})C_{i}^{(0)}(-t,\tau,\mu^{2})+\frac{g(\mu^{2})^{3}}{4\pi^{2}}C_{i}^{(0)}(-t,\tau,\mu^{2})\,c_{i}^{(1)}(-t,\tau,\mu^{2})+\dots

(361)

we are able to relate
C_{i}(-t,\tau,-t)
and
C_{i}(-t,\tau,\mu^{2})
.
To this end we need to solve eq. (
357
) perturbatively:
up to
{\cal{O}}\left[\alpha(\mu_{0}^{2})^{3}\right]
we have

\displaystyle a(\mu^{2})

\displaystyle=\frac{a(\mu_{0}^{2})\left(\frac{\mu_{0}^{2}}{\mu^{2}}\right)^{\epsilon}}{1+\frac{a(\mu_{0}^{2})}{\epsilon}\left[1-\left(\frac{\mu_{0}^{2}}{\mu^{2}}\right)^{\epsilon}\right]\left[b_{0}+\frac{a(\mu_{0}^{2})}{2}\,b_{1}\left(1+\left(\frac{\mu_{0}^{2}}{\mu^{2}}\right)^{\epsilon}\right)\right]}+{\cal{O}}\left[a(\mu_{0}^{2})^{4}\right].

(362)

We use the equation above to write the coupling

g(-t)=4\pi\sqrt{a(-t)}
, on the LHS of
eq. (
361
), in terms of

g(\mu^{2})
, obtaining

\displaystyle C_{g}^{(0)}(-t,\tau,\mu^{2})

\displaystyle=\left(\frac{\mu^{2}}{-t}\right)^{\frac{\epsilon}{2}}C_{g}^{(0)}(-t,\tau,-t),

(363a)

\displaystyle c_{g}^{(1)}(-t,\tau,\mu^{2})

\displaystyle=\left(\frac{\mu^{2}}{-t}\right)^{\epsilon}c_{g}^{(1)}(-t,\tau,-t)-\frac{b_{0}}{8\epsilon}\left[1-\left(\frac{\mu^{2}}{-t}\right)^{\epsilon}\right],

(363b)

\displaystyle c_{g}^{(2)}(-t,\tau,\mu^{2})

\displaystyle=\left(\frac{\mu^{2}}{-t}\right)^{2\epsilon}c_{g}^{(2)}(-t,\tau,-t)+\frac{3b_{0}^{2}}{128\epsilon^{2}}\left[1-\left(\frac{\mu^{2}}{-t}\right)^{\epsilon}\right]^{2}

\displaystyle\hskip-28.45274pt-\,\frac{1}{64\epsilon}\left[1-\left(\frac{\mu^{2}}{-t}\right)^{\epsilon}\right]\bigg\{b_{1}\left[1+\left(\frac{\mu^{2}}{-t}\right)^{\epsilon}\right]+24b_{0}\left(\frac{\mu^{2}}{-t}\right)^{\epsilon}c_{g}^{(1)}(-t,\tau,-t)\bigg\}.

(363c)

At this point we still need to
relate the impact factors
C_{i}(-t,\tau,-t)
,
where the factorization scale is generic, to
the impact factors evaluated at the scale

\tau=-t
. Once again, this can be easily
done by exploiting the scale invariance
of the amplitude. We obtain

C_{g}(\-t,\tau,-t)=C_{g}(-t,-t,-t)\,\left(\frac{-t}{\tau}\right)^{-C_{A}\frac{\alpha_{g}(-t,-t)}{2}},

(364)

which expanding to second
order in
\alpha_{s}
gives

\displaystyle C_{g}^{(0)}(-t,\tau,-t)

\displaystyle=C_{g}^{(0)}(-t,-t,-t),

(365a)

\displaystyle c_{g}^{(1)}(-t,\tau,-t)

\displaystyle=c_{g}^{(1)}-\frac{C_{A}\alpha_{g}^{(1)}}{2}\,\log\left(\frac{-t}{\tau}\right),

(365b)

\displaystyle c_{g}^{(2)}(-t,\tau,-t)

\displaystyle=c_{g}^{(2)}+\frac{1}{2}\left(\frac{C_{A}\alpha_{g}^{(1)}}{2}\log\frac{-t}{\tau}\right)^{2}-\frac{C_{A}}{2}\log\frac{-t}{\tau}\Big[c_{g}^{(1)}\,\alpha_{g}^{(1)}+\alpha_{g}^{(2)}\Big].

(365c)

where
c_{g}^{(1)}\equiv c_{g}^{(1)}(-t,-t,-t)
,

\alpha_{g}^{(1)}\equiv\alpha_{g}^{(1)}(-t,-t)
,
and similarly
c_{g}^{(2)}\equiv c_{g}^{(2)}(-t,-t,-t)
,

\alpha_{g}^{(2)}\equiv\alpha_{g}^{(2)}(-t,-t)
,
are given in the ancillary file of

[
23
]
. Combining
eq. (
363
) with
eq. (
365
) we obtain

\displaystyle C_{g}^{(0)}(-t,\tau,\mu^{2})

\displaystyle=\left(\frac{\mu^{2}}{-t}\right)^{\frac{\epsilon}{2}}C_{g}^{(0)}(-t,-t,-t),

(366a)

\displaystyle c_{g}^{(1)}(-t,\tau,\mu^{2})

\displaystyle=\left(\frac{\mu^{2}}{-t}\right)^{\epsilon}\left[c_{g}^{(1)}-\frac{C_{A}\,\alpha_{g}^{(1)}}{2}\log\left(\frac{-t}{\tau}\right)\right]-\frac{b_{0}}{8\epsilon}\left[1-\left(\frac{\mu^{2}}{-t}\right)^{\epsilon}\right],

(366b)

\displaystyle c_{g}^{(2)}(-t,\tau,\mu^{2})

\displaystyle=\left(\frac{\mu^{2}}{-t}\right)^{2\epsilon}\bigg\{c_{g}^{(2)}+\frac{C_{A}^{2}}{8}\big(\alpha_{g}^{(1)}\big)^{2}\log^{2}\left(\frac{-t}{\tau}\right)

\displaystyle\hskip 56.9055pt-\,\frac{C_{A}}{2}\log\left(\frac{-t}{\tau}\right)\Big(c_{g}^{(1)}\,\alpha_{g}^{(1)}+\alpha_{g}^{(2)}\Big)\bigg\}

\displaystyle\hskip 0.0pt-\,\frac{1}{64\epsilon}\left[1-\left(\frac{\mu^{2}}{-t}\right)^{\epsilon}\right]\bigg\{b_{1}\left[1+\left(\frac{\mu^{2}}{-t}\right)^{\epsilon}\right]

\displaystyle\hskip 56.9055pt+\,24b_{0}\left(\frac{\mu^{2}}{-t}\right)^{\epsilon}\bigg[c_{g}^{(1)}-\frac{C_{A}\,\alpha_{g}^{(1)}}{2}\,\log\left(\frac{-t}{\tau}\right)\bigg]\bigg\}

\displaystyle\hskip 0.0pt+\,\frac{3}{128\epsilon^{2}}b_{0}^{2}\left[1-\left(\frac{\mu^{2}}{-t}\right)^{\epsilon}\right]^{2}.

(366c)

Repeating the same procedure for the
Regge trajectory we find

\displaystyle\alpha_{g}^{(1)}(-t,\mu^{2})

\displaystyle=\alpha_{g}^{(1)}(-t,-t)\left(\frac{\mu^{2}}{-t}\right)^{\epsilon},

(367a)

\displaystyle\alpha_{g}^{(2)}(-t,\mu^{2})

\displaystyle=\alpha_{g}^{(2)}(-t,-t)\left(\frac{\mu^{2}}{-t}\right)^{2\epsilon}-\alpha_{g}^{(1)}(-t,-t)\left(\frac{\mu^{2}}{-t}\right)^{\epsilon}\,\frac{b_{0}}{4\epsilon}\left[1-\left(\frac{\mu^{2}}{-t}\right)^{\epsilon}\right].

(367b)

Once the scale dependence for the impact
factors and Regge trajectory has been obtained,
it can be used in the factorization formula for
the
2\to 3
scattering amplitudes (eq. (
71
)
in the main text) to derive the scale dependence
of the Lipatov vertex. After some work we find
that the Lipatov vertex evaluated at a generic
renormalization scale
\mu^{2}
is related to the
vertex at the scale
\mu^{2}=|\mathbf{p}_{4}|^{2}

as follows:

\displaystyle V_{0}(t_{1},t_{2},|\mathbf{p}_{4}|^{2},\tau,\mu^{2})

\displaystyle=V_{0}(t_{1},t_{2},|\mathbf{p}_{4}|^{2},\tau,|\mathbf{p}_{4}|^{2})\left(\frac{\mu^{2}}{|\mathbf{p}_{4}|^{2}}\right)^{\frac{\epsilon}{2}},

(368a)

\displaystyle v^{(1)}(t_{1},t_{2},|\mathbf{p}_{4}|^{2},\tau,\mu^{2})

\displaystyle=v^{(1)}(t_{1},t_{2},|\mathbf{p}_{4}|^{2},\tau,|\mathbf{p}_{4}|^{2})\left(\frac{\mu^{2}}{|\mathbf{p}_{4}|^{2}}\right)^{\epsilon}-\frac{b_{0}}{8\epsilon}\left[1-\left(\frac{\mu^{2}}{|\mathbf{p}_{4}|^{2}}\right)^{\epsilon}\right],

(368b)

\displaystyle v^{(2)}(t_{1},t_{2},|\mathbf{p}_{4}|^{2},\tau,\mu^{2})

\displaystyle=v^{(2)}(t_{1},t_{2},|\mathbf{p}_{4}|^{2},\tau,|\mathbf{p}_{4}|^{2})\left(\frac{\mu^{2}}{|\mathbf{p}_{4}|^{2}}\right)^{2\epsilon}+\frac{3\,b_{0}^{2}}{128\epsilon^{2}}\left[\left(\frac{\mu^{2}}{|\mathbf{p}_{4}|^{2}}\right)^{\epsilon}-1\right]^{2}

\displaystyle\hskip-99.58464pt-\,\frac{1}{64\epsilon}\left[1-\left(\frac{\mu^{2}}{|\mathbf{p}_{4}|^{2}}\right)^{\epsilon}\right]\left\{b_{1}\left[1+\left(\frac{\mu^{2}}{|\mathbf{p}_{4}|^{2}}\right)^{\epsilon}\right]+24b_{0}\left(\frac{\mu^{2}}{|\mathbf{p}_{4}|^{2}}\right)^{\epsilon}v^{(1)}(t_{1},t_{2},|\mathbf{p}_{4}|^{2},\tau,|\mathbf{p}_{4}|^{2})\right\}.

(368c)

Furthermore, the relation between
the Lipatov vertex evaluated at a
generic factorization scale
\tau

and the vertex at
\tau=|\mathbf{p}_{4}|^{2}

is given by

\displaystyle V(t_{1},t_{2},|\mathbf{p}_{4}|^{2},\tau,\mu^{2})=V(t_{1},t_{2},|\mathbf{p}_{4}|^{2},|\mathbf{p}_{4}|^{2},\mu^{2})\left(\frac{|\mathbf{p}_{4}|^{2}}{\tau}\right)^{-\frac{1}{2}\big[\alpha_{g}(t_{1},\mu^{2})+\alpha_{g}(t_{2},\mu^{2})\big]},

(369)

and expanding to second order
in
\alpha_{s}
we obtain

\displaystyle V_{0}(t_{1},t_{2},|\mathbf{p}_{4}|^{2},\tau,|\mathbf{p}_{4}|^{2})

\displaystyle=V_{0}(t_{1},t_{2},|\mathbf{p}_{4}|^{2},|\mathbf{p}_{4}|^{2},|\mathbf{p}_{4}|^{2}),

(370a)

\displaystyle v^{(1)}(t_{1},t_{2},|\mathbf{p}_{4}|^{2},\tau,|\mathbf{p}_{4}|^{2})

\displaystyle=v^{(1)}(t_{1},t_{2},|\mathbf{p}_{4}|^{2})

\displaystyle\hskip-56.9055pt-\,\frac{\alpha_{g}^{(1)}C_{A}}{2}\log\frac{|\mathbf{p}_{4}|^{2}}{\tau}\left[\left(\frac{|\mathbf{p}_{4}|^{2}}{-t_{1}}\right)^{\epsilon}+\left(\frac{|\mathbf{p}_{4}|^{2}}{-t_{2}}\right)^{\epsilon}\right],

(370b)

\displaystyle v^{(2)}(t_{1},t_{2},|\mathbf{p}_{4}|^{2},\tau,|\mathbf{p}_{4}|^{2})

\displaystyle=v^{(2)}(t_{1},t_{2},|\mathbf{p}_{4}|^{2})

\displaystyle\hskip-56.9055pt-\,v^{(1)}(t_{1},t_{2},|\mathbf{p}_{4}|^{2})\frac{\alpha_{g}^{(1)}C_{A}}{2}\log\frac{|\mathbf{p}_{4}|^{2}}{\tau}\left[\left(\frac{|\mathbf{p}_{4}|^{2}}{-t_{1}}\right)^{\epsilon}+\left(\frac{|\mathbf{p}_{4}|^{2}}{-t_{2}}\right)^{\epsilon}\right]

\displaystyle\hskip-56.9055pt-\,\frac{\alpha_{g}^{(1)}C_{A}b_{0}}{8\epsilon}\log\frac{|\mathbf{p}_{4}|^{2}}{\tau}\left[\left(\frac{|\mathbf{p}_{4}|^{2}}{-t_{1}}\right)^{2\epsilon}+\left(\frac{|\mathbf{p}_{4}|^{2}}{-t_{2}}\right)^{2\epsilon}-\left(\frac{|\mathbf{p}_{4}|^{2}}{-t_{1}}\right)^{\epsilon}-\left(\frac{|\mathbf{p}_{4}|^{2}}{-t_{2}}\right)^{\epsilon}\right]

\displaystyle\hskip-56.9055pt+\,\frac{C_{A}}{8}\log\frac{|\mathbf{p}_{4}|^{2}}{\tau}\Bigg\{(\alpha_{g}^{(1)})^{2}C_{A}\log\frac{|\mathbf{p}_{4}|^{2}}{\tau}\bigg[\left(\frac{|\mathbf{p}_{4}|^{2}}{-t_{1}}\right)^{\epsilon}+\left(\frac{|\mathbf{p}_{4}|^{2}}{-t_{2}}\right)^{\epsilon}\bigg]^{2}

\displaystyle\hskip 0.0pt-\,4\alpha_{g}^{(2)}\bigg[\left(\frac{|\mathbf{p}_{4}|^{2}}{-t_{1}}\right)^{2\epsilon}+\left(\frac{|\mathbf{p}_{4}|^{2}}{-t_{2}}\right)^{2\epsilon}\bigg]\Bigg\}

(370c)

Combining eq. (
368
)
with eq. (
370
) we have

\displaystyle V_{0}(t_{1},t_{2},|\mathbf{p}_{4}|^{2},\tau,\mu^{2})

\displaystyle=V_{0}(t_{1},t_{2},|\mathbf{p}_{4}|^{2},|\mathbf{p}_{4}|^{2},|\mathbf{p}_{4}|^{2})\left(\frac{\mu^{2}}{|\mathbf{p}_{4}|^{2}}\right)^{\frac{\epsilon}{2}},

(371a)

\displaystyle v^{(1)}(t_{1},t_{2},|\mathbf{p}_{4}|^{2},\tau,\mu^{2})

\displaystyle=v^{(1)}(t_{1},t_{2},|\mathbf{p}_{4}|^{2})\left(\frac{\mu^{2}}{|\mathbf{p}_{4}|^{2}}\right)^{\epsilon}+\frac{b_{0}}{8\epsilon}\left[\left(\frac{\mu^{2}}{|\mathbf{p}_{4}|^{2}}\right)^{\epsilon}-1\right]

\displaystyle\hskip 28.45274pt-\,\frac{\alpha_{g}^{(1)}C_{A}}{2}\log\frac{|\mathbf{p}_{4}|^{2}}{\tau}\left[\left(\frac{|\mathbf{p}_{4}|^{2}}{-t_{1}}\right)^{\epsilon}+\left(\frac{|\mathbf{p}_{4}|^{2}}{-t_{2}}\right)^{\epsilon}\right],

(371b)

\displaystyle v^{(2)}(t_{1},t_{2},|\mathbf{p}_{4}|^{2},\tau,\mu^{2})

\displaystyle=v^{(2)}(t_{1},t_{2},|\mathbf{p}_{4}|^{2})\left(\frac{\mu^{2}}{|\mathbf{p}_{4}|^{2}}\right)^{2\epsilon}

\displaystyle\hskip-56.9055pt+\,v^{(1)}(t_{1},t_{2},|\mathbf{p}_{4}|^{2})\Bigg\{\frac{3b_{0}}{8\epsilon}\left(\frac{\mu^{2}}{|\mathbf{p}_{4}|^{2}}\right)^{\epsilon}\left[\left(\frac{\mu^{2}}{|\mathbf{p}_{4}|^{2}}\right)^{\epsilon}-1\right]

\displaystyle\hskip 0.0pt-\,\frac{\alpha_{g}^{(1)}C_{A}}{2}\log\frac{|\mathbf{p}_{4}|^{2}}{\tau}\left(\frac{\mu^{2}}{|\mathbf{p}_{4}|^{2}}\right)^{2\epsilon}\bigg[\left(\frac{|\mathbf{p}_{4}|^{2}}{-t_{1}}\right)^{\epsilon}+\left(\frac{|\mathbf{p}_{4}|^{2}}{-t_{2}}\right)^{\epsilon}\bigg]\Bigg\}

\displaystyle\hskip-56.9055pt+\,\frac{3b_{0}^{2}}{128\epsilon^{2}}\Bigg[\left(\frac{\mu^{2}}{|\mathbf{p}_{4}|^{2}}\right)^{\epsilon}-1\Bigg]^{2}+\frac{b_{1}}{64\epsilon}\Bigg[\left(\frac{\mu^{2}}{|\mathbf{p}_{4}|^{2}}\right)^{2\epsilon}-1\Bigg]

\displaystyle\hskip-56.9055pt-\,\frac{\alpha_{g}^{(1)}C_{A}b_{0}}{16\epsilon}\left(\frac{\mu^{2}}{|\mathbf{p}_{4}|^{2}}\right)^{\epsilon}\log\frac{|\mathbf{p}_{4}|^{2}}{\tau}\Bigg\{\left(\frac{\mu^{2}}{|\mathbf{p}_{4}|^{2}}\right)^{\epsilon}\bigg[\left(\frac{|\mathbf{p}_{4}|^{2}}{-t_{1}}\right)^{\epsilon}+\left(\frac{|\mathbf{p}_{4}|^{2}}{-t_{2}}\right)^{\epsilon}

\displaystyle\hskip 0.0pt+\,2\left(\frac{|\mathbf{p}_{4}|^{2}}{-t_{1}}\right)^{2\epsilon}+\,2\left(\frac{|\mathbf{p}_{4}|^{2}}{-t_{2}}\right)^{2\epsilon}\bigg]-3\bigg[\left(\frac{|\mathbf{p}_{4}|^{2}}{-t_{1}}\right)^{\epsilon}+\left(\frac{|\mathbf{p}_{4}|^{2}}{-t_{2}}\right)^{\epsilon}\bigg]\Bigg\}

\displaystyle\hskip-56.9055pt+\,\frac{C_{A}}{8}\left(\frac{\mu^{2}}{|\mathbf{p}_{4}|^{2}}\right)^{2\epsilon}\log\frac{|\mathbf{p}_{4}|^{2}}{\tau}\Bigg\{C_{A}\,\big(\alpha_{g}^{(1)}\big)^{2}\log\frac{|\mathbf{p}_{4}|^{2}}{\tau}\bigg[\left(\frac{|\mathbf{p}_{4}|^{2}}{-t_{1}}\right)^{\epsilon}+\left(\frac{|\mathbf{p}_{4}|^{2}}{-t_{2}}\right)^{\epsilon}\bigg]^{2}

\displaystyle\hskip 0.0pt-4\alpha_{g}^{(2)}\bigg[\left(\frac{|\mathbf{p}_{4}|^{2}}{-t_{1}}\right)^{2\epsilon}+\left(\frac{|\mathbf{p}_{4}|^{2}}{-t_{2}}\right)^{2\epsilon}\bigg]\Bigg\}.

(371c)

Appendix E
The dipole formula in multi-Regge kinematics

According to the infrared factorization theorem
an
n
-particle scattering amplitude has the
following multiplicative structure
[
132
,
133
,
134
,
135
,
136
,
137
,
138
,
139
,
140
,
141
,
142
,
143
,
23
]

{\cal M}_{n}\left(\{p_{i}\},\mu,\alpha_{s}(\mu^{2})\right)={\bf Z}_{n}\left(\{p_{i}\},\mu,\alpha_{s}(\mu^{2})\right){\cal H}_{n}\left(\{p_{i}\},\mu,\alpha_{s}(\mu^{2})\right),

(372)

where on the left-hand side one has the
infrared divergent
n
-particle amplitude
in dimensional regularization
{\cal M}_{n}
,
while on the right-hand side
{\cal H}_{n}
is
the finite reminder, and infrared divergences
are factorized in the renormalization factor

{\bf Z}_{n}
, which has the exponential structure

{\bf Z}_{n}\left(\{p_{i}\},\mu,\alpha_{s}(\mu^{2})\right)={\cal P}\exp\left\{-\frac{1}{2}\int_{0}^{\mu^{2}}\frac{d\lambda^{2}}{\lambda^{2}}\,{\bf\Gamma}_{n}\left(\{p_{i}\},\lambda,\alpha_{s}(\lambda^{2})\right)\right\}\,,

(373)

and in turn
{\bf\Gamma}_{n}
represents the
soft anomalous dimension for scattering of
massless partons (
p_{i}^{2}=0
). Through three
loops, it is given by

{\bf\Gamma}_{n}\left(\{p_{i}\},\lambda,\alpha_{s}(\lambda^{2})\right)={\bf\Gamma}_{n}^{\rm dip.}\left(\{p_{i}\},\lambda,\alpha_{s}(\lambda^{2})\right)+{\bf\Delta}_{n}\left(\{\rho_{ijkl}\}\right),

(374)

where

{\bf\Gamma}_{n}^{\rm dip.}\left(\{p_{i}\},\lambda,\alpha_{s}(\lambda^{2})\right)=-\frac{\gamma_{K}(\alpha_{s})}{2}\sum_{i<j}\log\left(\frac{-s_{ij}}{\lambda^{2}}\right)\,\mathbf{T}_{i}\cdot\mathbf{T}_{j}\,+\,\sum_{i}\gamma_{i}(\alpha_{s})\,,

(375)

and the argument of the functions indicates that
the dependence on the scale is both explicit and
via the
4-2\epsilon
dimensional coupling, which
obeys the renormalization group equation

\beta(\alpha_{s},\epsilon)\equiv\frac{d\alpha_{s}}{d\ln\mu}=-2\epsilon\,\alpha_{s}-\frac{\alpha_{s}^{2}}{2\pi}\sum_{n=0}^{\infty}b_{n}\,\left(\frac{\alpha_{s}}{\pi}\right)^{n}\,,

(376)

with
b_{0}=\frac{11}{3}C_{A}-\frac{2}{3}n_{f}
.

Aim of this appendix is to specialise eq. (
374
)
to the
2\to 3
parton amplitudes considered in this paper.
We focus in particular on the dipole component, eq. (
375
),
and neglect the quadrupole term
{\bf\Delta}_{n}\left(\{\rho_{ijkl}\}\right)
.
The latter starts at three loops and therefore it is not relevant
for the analysis of the high-energy limit considered in the main
text, which concerns scattering amplitudes up to two loops. For
the purpose of this section we follow closely

[
56
,
75
]
and parameterize the
external momenta
P_{1},\dots,P_{5}
in the lab frame as follows:

P_{1}=(0,x_{1}\sqrt{S},\mathbf{0}),\qquad\qquad P_{2}=(x_{2}\sqrt{S},0,\mathbf{0}),

(377)

where
S
is the hadronic centre of mass energy,
such that
s_{12}=x_{1}x_{2}S
, and

P_{i}=(|\mathbf{p}_{i}|\,e^{y_{i}},|\mathbf{p}_{i}|\,e^{-y_{i}},\mathbf{p}_{i}),\qquad{\rm with}\qquad i=3,4,5.

(378)

In turn, the transverse momenta
\mathbf{p}_{i}

are parameterized as
\mathbf{p}_{i}=|\mathbf{p}_{i}|(\cos\phi_{i},\sin\phi_{i})
, where

\phi_{i}
is the azimuthal angle between the vector

\mathbf{p}_{i}
and an arbitrary vector in the
transverse plane. Momentum conservation requires

\displaystyle\mathbf{0}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\mathbf{p}_{3}+\mathbf{p}_{4}+\mathbf{p}_{5},

\displaystyle x_{1}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\frac{1}{\sqrt{S}}\Big(|\mathbf{p}_{3}|e^{-y_{3}}+|\mathbf{p}_{4}|e^{-y_{4}}+|\mathbf{p}_{5}|e^{-y_{5}}\Big),

(379)

\displaystyle x_{2}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\frac{1}{\sqrt{S}}\Big(|\mathbf{p}_{3}|e^{y_{3}}+|\mathbf{p}_{4}|e^{y_{4}}+|\mathbf{p}_{5}|e^{y_{5}}\Big),

and the kinematic invariants of eq. (
7
) reads

\displaystyle s_{12}=x_{1}x_{2}S

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\sum_{i,j=3}^{5}|\mathbf{p}_{i}||\mathbf{p}_{j}|\,e^{y_{i}-y_{j}},

\displaystyle s_{1i}=-2P_{1}\cdot P_{i}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle-\sum_{j=3}^{5}|\mathbf{p}_{i}||\mathbf{p}_{j}|\,e^{y_{i}-y_{j}},\qquad i=3,4,5,

\displaystyle s_{2i}=-2P_{2}\cdot P_{i}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle-\sum_{j=3}^{5}|\mathbf{p}_{i}||\mathbf{p}_{j}|\,e^{-(y_{i}-y_{j})},\qquad i=3,4,5

(380)

\displaystyle s_{ij}=2P_{i}\cdot P_{j}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle|\mathbf{p}_{i}||\mathbf{p}_{j}|\big[\cosh(y_{i}-y_{j})-\cos(\phi_{i}-\phi_{j})\big],\qquad i,j=3,4,5.

In terms of the rapidity variables
y_{i}
, the
multi-Regge limit of eq. (
6
) reads

y_{3}\gg y_{4}\gg y_{5}.

(381)

In this limit
x_{1}\simeq|\mathbf{p}_{5}|/\sqrt{S}\,e^{-y_{5}}
,

x_{2}\simeq|\mathbf{p}_{3}|/\sqrt{S}\,e^{y_{3}}
, and the
invariants of eq. (
380
) can be approximated as

\displaystyle s_{12}

\displaystyle\simeq

\displaystyle|\mathbf{p}_{3}||\mathbf{p}_{5}|\,e^{y_{3}-y_{5}},

\displaystyle s_{1i}

\displaystyle\simeq

\displaystyle-|\mathbf{p}_{i}||\mathbf{p}_{5}|\,e^{y_{i}-y_{5}},\qquad i=3,4,5

\displaystyle s_{2i}

\displaystyle\simeq

\displaystyle-|\mathbf{p}_{3}||\mathbf{p}_{i}|\,e^{y_{3}-y_{i}},\qquad i=3,4,5

(382)

\displaystyle s_{ij}

\displaystyle\simeq

\displaystyle|\mathbf{p}_{i}||\mathbf{p}_{j}|\,e^{|y_{i}-y_{j}|},\qquad i,j=3,4,5.

Specialising eq. (
375
) to the case
n=5

and taking the high-energy limit according to
eq. (
382
) one has

\displaystyle{\bf\Gamma}_{5}^{\rm dip.}\left(\{p_{i}\},\lambda,\alpha_{s}(\lambda^{2})\right)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle-\frac{\gamma_{K}(\alpha_{s})}{2}\bigg\{\bigg[\log\frac{|\mathbf{p}_{3}|}{\lambda}+\log\frac{|\mathbf{p}_{5}|}{\lambda}-i\pi+y_{3}-y_{5}\bigg]\,\mathbf{T}_{1}\cdot\mathbf{T}_{2}

(383)

\displaystyle\hskip 14.22636pt+\,\bigg[\log\frac{|\mathbf{p}_{3}|}{\lambda}+\log\frac{|\mathbf{p}_{5}|}{\lambda}+y_{3}-y_{5}\bigg]\,\mathbf{T}_{1}\cdot\mathbf{T}_{3}

\displaystyle\hskip 14.22636pt+\,\bigg[\log\frac{|\mathbf{p}_{4}|}{\lambda}+\log\frac{|\mathbf{p}_{5}|}{\lambda}+y_{4}-y_{5}\bigg]\,\mathbf{T}_{1}\cdot\mathbf{T}_{4}

\displaystyle\hskip 14.22636pt+\,2\log\frac{|\mathbf{p}_{5}|}{\lambda}\,\mathbf{T}_{1}\cdot\mathbf{T}_{5}+2\log\frac{|\mathbf{p}_{3}|}{\lambda}\,\mathbf{T}_{2}\cdot\mathbf{T}_{3}

\displaystyle\hskip 14.22636pt+\,\bigg[\log\frac{|\mathbf{p}_{3}|}{\lambda}+\log\frac{|\mathbf{p}_{4}|}{\lambda}+y_{3}-y_{4}\bigg]\,\mathbf{T}_{2}\cdot\mathbf{T}_{4}

\displaystyle\hskip 14.22636pt+\,\bigg[\log\frac{|\mathbf{p}_{3}|}{\lambda}+\log\frac{|\mathbf{p}_{3}|}{\lambda}+y_{3}-y_{5}\bigg]\,\mathbf{T}_{2}\cdot\mathbf{T}_{5}

\displaystyle\hskip 14.22636pt+\,\bigg[\log\frac{|\mathbf{p}_{3}|}{\lambda}+\log\frac{|\mathbf{p}_{4}|}{\lambda}-i\pi+y_{3}-y_{4}\bigg]\,\mathbf{T}_{3}\cdot\mathbf{T}_{4}

\displaystyle\hskip 14.22636pt+\,\bigg[\log\frac{|\mathbf{p}_{3}|}{\lambda}+\log\frac{|\mathbf{p}_{5}|}{\lambda}-i\pi+y_{3}-y_{5}\bigg]\,\mathbf{T}_{3}\cdot\mathbf{T}_{5}

\displaystyle\hskip 14.22636pt+\,\bigg[\log\frac{|\mathbf{p}_{4}|}{\lambda}+\log\frac{|\mathbf{p}_{5}|}{\lambda}-i\pi+y_{4}-y_{5}\bigg]\,\mathbf{T}_{4}\cdot\mathbf{T}_{5}\Bigg\}+\sum_{i=1}^{5}\gamma_{i}(\alpha_{s})\,.

The expression in eq. (
383
)
can be expressed in terms of the colour operators
in eqs. (
32
), (
33
)
and (
35
) by means of the identities

\sum_{i=1}^{5}{\bf T}_{i}=0,\qquad\qquad\Big(\sum_{i=1}^{5}{\bf T}_{i}\Big)^{2}=\sum_{i=1}^{5}C_{i}+2\sum_{j>i=1}^{5}{\bf T}_{i}\cdot{\bf T}_{j}.

(384)

After some elaboration one has

\displaystyle{\bf\Gamma}_{5}^{\rm dip.}\left(\{p_{i}\},\lambda,\alpha_{s}(\lambda^{2})\right)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle-\frac{\gamma_{K}(\alpha_{s})}{2}\bigg[-\big(y_{4}-y_{5}\big)\mathbf{T}_{t_{1}}^{2}-\big(y_{3}-y_{4}\big)\mathbf{T}_{t_{2}}^{2}-i\pi\,\mathbf{T}_{s}^{2}

(385)

\displaystyle\hskip 28.45274pt-\frac{1}{2}\bigg(\log\frac{|\mathbf{p}_{5}|^{2}}{\lambda^{2}}-i\pi\bigg)\big(C_{1}+C_{5}\big)-\frac{1}{2}\bigg(\log\frac{|\mathbf{p}_{4}|^{2}}{\lambda^{2}}-i\pi\bigg)C_{4}

\displaystyle\hskip 28.45274pt-\frac{1}{2}\bigg(\log\frac{|\mathbf{p}_{3}|^{2}}{\lambda^{2}}-i\pi\bigg)\big(C_{2}+C_{3}\big)\bigg]+\sum_{i=1}^{5}\gamma_{i}(\alpha_{s})\,,

which is consistent with eq. (6.8) of

[
75
]
. Using eq. (
41
)
it is possible to write the dipole formula
in terms of the operators
\mathbf{T}_{(\pm,\pm)}

introduced in eq. (
40
), in such a way
to make symmetries under the exchanges

2\leftrightarrow 3
and
1\leftrightarrow 5

manifest. Taking into account that in the
high-energy limit
C_{1}=C_{5}\equiv C_{i}
,

C_{2}=C_{3}\equiv C_{j}
, and identifying

C_{4}\equiv C_{v}=C_{A}
, one obtains

\displaystyle{\bf\Gamma}_{5}^{\rm dip.}\left(\{p_{i}\},\lambda,\alpha_{s}(\lambda^{2})\right)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle-\frac{\gamma_{K}(\alpha_{s})}{2}\bigg[-\big(y_{4}-y_{5}\big)\mathbf{T}_{t_{1}}^{2}-\big(y_{3}-y_{4}\big)\mathbf{T}_{t_{2}}^{2}

(386)

\displaystyle\hskip-28.45274pt-\,\frac{i\pi}{2}\bigg(\mathbf{T}_{(++)}+\mathbf{T}_{(+-)}+\mathbf{T}_{(-+)}+\mathbf{T}_{(--)}\bigg)

\displaystyle\hskip-28.45274pt-C_{i}\log\frac{|\mathbf{p}_{5}|^{2}}{\lambda^{2}}-C_{j}\log\frac{|\mathbf{p}_{3}|^{2}}{\lambda^{2}}-\frac{C_{v}}{2}\bigg(\log\frac{|\mathbf{p}_{4}|^{2}}{\lambda^{2}}-i\pi\bigg)\bigg]+\sum_{i=1}^{5}\gamma_{i}(\alpha_{s})\,.

The operator
\mathbf{T}_{(++)}
commutes
with
\mathbf{T}^{2}_{t_{1}}
and
\mathbf{T}^{2}_{t_{2}}
,
furthermore one has

\mathbf{T}_{(++)}=-\frac{1}{2}\big(\mathbf{T}^{2}_{t_{1}}+\mathbf{T}^{2}_{t_{2}}-C_{4}\big).

(387)

This relation can be used in
eq. (
386
) to obtain

\displaystyle{\bf\Gamma}_{5}^{\rm dip.}\left(\{p_{i}\},\lambda,\alpha_{s}(\lambda^{2})\right)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle-\frac{\gamma_{K}(\alpha_{s})}{2}\bigg[-\bigg(y_{4}-y_{5}-\frac{i\pi}{4}\bigg)\mathbf{T}_{t_{1}}^{2}-\bigg(y_{3}-y_{4}-\frac{i\pi}{4}\bigg)\mathbf{T}_{t_{2}}^{2}

(388)

\displaystyle\hskip-28.45274pt-\,\frac{i\pi}{2}\bigg(\mathbf{T}_{(+-)}+\mathbf{T}_{(-+)}+\mathbf{T}_{(--)}\bigg)

\displaystyle\hskip-28.45274pt-C_{i}\log\frac{|\mathbf{p}_{5}|^{2}}{\lambda^{2}}-C_{j}\log\frac{|\mathbf{p}_{3}|^{2}}{\lambda^{2}}-\frac{C_{v}}{2}\bigg(\log\frac{|\mathbf{p}_{4}|^{2}}{\lambda^{2}}-\frac{i\pi}{2}\bigg)\bigg]+\sum_{i=1}^{5}\gamma_{i}(\alpha_{s})\,.

Using this result it appears natural, in the high-energy
limit, to factorize the dipole renormalization factor

{\bf Z}_{5}^{\rm dip.}
in eq. (
373
) as follows:

\displaystyle{\bf Z}_{5}^{\rm dip.}\left(\{p_{i}\},\mu,\alpha_{s}(\mu^{2})\right)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle Z_{i}\bigg(\frac{|\mathbf{p}_{5}|}{\mu},\alpha_{s}(\mu^{2}),\epsilon\bigg)\,Z_{j}\bigg(\frac{|\mathbf{p}_{3}|}{\mu},\alpha_{s}(\mu^{2}),\epsilon\bigg)

(389)

\displaystyle\hskip 0.0pt\times\,Z_{v}\bigg(\frac{p_{4\perp}}{\mu},\alpha_{s}(\mu^{2}),\epsilon\bigg)\,{\bf\hat{Z}}\Big(\{y_{i}-y_{j}\},\alpha_{s}(\mu^{2}),\epsilon\Big).

In this equation the last
factor
{\bf\hat{Z}}
reads

\displaystyle{\bf\hat{Z}}\Big(\{y_{i}-y_{j}\},\alpha_{s}(\mu^{2}),\epsilon\Big)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\exp\Bigg\{K\Big(\alpha_{s}(\mu^{2}),\epsilon\Big)\bigg[\tilde{\eta}_{1}\,\mathbf{T}^{2}_{t_{1}}+\tilde{\eta}_{2}\,\mathbf{T}^{2}_{t_{2}}

(390)

\displaystyle\hskip 28.45274pt+\,\frac{i\pi}{2}\Big(\mathbf{T}_{(+-)}+\mathbf{T}_{(-+)}+\mathbf{T}_{(--)}\Big)\bigg]\Bigg\}\,,

where

\tilde{\eta}_{1}=y_{4}-y_{5}-\frac{i\pi}{4},\qquad\qquad\tilde{\eta}_{2}=y_{3}-y_{4}-\frac{i\pi}{4},

(391)

and the factor
K(\alpha_{s}(\mu^{2}),\epsilon)

is the well-known integral over the
scale of the cusp anomalous dimension:

K\Big(\alpha_{s}(\mu^{2}),\epsilon\Big)=-\frac{1}{4}\int_{0}^{\mu^{2}}\frac{d\lambda^{2}}{\lambda^{2}}\,\hat{\gamma}_{K}\left(\alpha_{s}(\lambda^{2})\right)\,.

(392)

The rapidity differences
\tilde{\eta}_{1}
,
\tilde{\eta}_{2}

in eq. (
391
) constitutes the natural
generalization of the signature-even combination
of logarithms
L=\log(s/|t|)-i\pi/2
,
introduced in case of
2\to 2
parton scattering
(cf. with eq. (2.9) of
[
23
]
).
The relation with the rapidity factors in
eq. (
72
) can be easily found to be

\displaystyle\eta_{1}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\frac{1}{2}\big[\log(1-z)+\log(1-\bar{z})\big]+\log\frac{|\mathbf{p}_{4}|^{2}}{\tau}+\tilde{\eta}_{1},

\displaystyle\eta_{2}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\frac{1}{2}\big[\log(z)+\log(\bar{z})\big]+\log\frac{|\mathbf{p}_{4}|^{2}}{\tau}+\tilde{\eta}_{2}.

(393)

Let us also point out that in this section
we keep explicit dependence on the IR renormalisation
scale
\mu
, and fix the Regge factorization scale
to
\tau=|\mathbf{p}_{4}|^{2}
. Next, we define

\gamma_{i}(\alpha_{s})\equiv\gamma_{1}(\alpha_{s})=\gamma_{5}(\alpha_{s}),\qquad\gamma_{j}(\alpha_{s})\equiv\gamma_{2}(\alpha_{s})=\gamma_{3}(\alpha_{s}),

(394)

such that the factors
Z_{i/j}
in
eq. (
389
) reads

Z_{i/j}\bigg(\frac{|\mathbf{p}_{5/3}|}{\mu},\alpha_{s}(\mu^{2}),\epsilon\bigg)=\exp\left\{-\frac{1}{2}\int_{0}^{\mu^{2}}\frac{d\lambda^{2}}{\lambda^{2}}\left[C_{i/j}\frac{\gamma_{K}(\alpha_{s})}{2}\log\frac{|\mathbf{p}_{5/3}|^{2}}{\lambda^{2}}+2\gamma_{i/j}(\alpha_{s})\right]\right\}\,,

(395)

and correspond to the collinear
singularities associated to the
impact factors
C_{i/j}
, according
to eqs. (
134
) and (
135
).
Last, the factor
Z_{v}
in
eq. (
389
) reads

Z_{v}\bigg(\frac{|\mathbf{p}_{4}|}{\mu},\alpha_{s}(\mu^{2}),\epsilon\bigg)=\exp\Bigg\{-\frac{1}{2}\int_{0}^{\mu^{2}}\frac{d\lambda^{2}}{\lambda^{2}}\bigg[C_{v}\frac{\gamma_{K}(\alpha_{s})}{4}\bigg(\log\frac{|\mathbf{p}_{4}|^{2}}{\lambda^{2}}-\frac{i\pi}{2}\bigg)+\gamma_{v}(\alpha_{s})\bigg]\Bigg\}\,,

(396)

where we identified

\gamma_{v}(\alpha_{s})\equiv\gamma_{4}(\alpha_{s}).

(397)

Eqs. (
395
) and (
396
) can be
expressed in terms of the factors

\displaystyle K_{D}\Big(\alpha_{s}(\mu^{2}),\epsilon\Big)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle-\frac{1}{4}\int_{0}^{\mu^{2}}\frac{d\lambda^{2}}{\lambda^{2}}\,\hat{\gamma}_{K}\left(\alpha_{s}(\lambda^{2})\right)\log\frac{\mu^{2}}{\lambda^{2}}\,,

(398)

\displaystyle K_{B_{i}}\Big(\alpha_{s}(\mu^{2}),\epsilon\Big)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle-\frac{1}{2}\int_{0}^{\mu^{2}}\frac{d\lambda^{2}}{\lambda^{2}}\,\hat{\gamma}_{i}\left(\alpha_{s}(\lambda^{2})\right)\,.

(399)

Then we have

\displaystyle Z_{i/j}\bigg(\frac{|\mathbf{p}_{5/3}|}{\mu},\alpha_{s}(\mu^{2}),\epsilon\bigg)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\exp\Bigg\{\bigg[K\Big(\alpha_{s}(\mu^{2}),\epsilon\Big)\log\frac{|\mathbf{p}_{5/3}|^{2}}{\mu^{2}}

(400)

\displaystyle\hskip 28.45274pt+\,K_{D}\Big(\alpha_{s}(\mu^{2}),\epsilon\Big)\bigg]C_{i/j}+2K_{B_{i}}\Big(\alpha_{s}(\mu^{2}),\epsilon\Big)\Bigg\}\,,

and

\displaystyle Z_{v}\bigg(\frac{|\mathbf{p}_{4}|}{\mu},\alpha_{s}(\mu^{2}),\epsilon\bigg)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\exp\Bigg\{\bigg[K\Big(\alpha_{s}(\mu^{2}),\epsilon\Big)\bigg(\log\frac{|\mathbf{p}_{4}|^{2}}{\lambda^{2}}-\frac{i\pi}{2}\bigg)

(401)

\displaystyle\hskip 28.45274pt+\,K_{D}\Big(\alpha_{s}(\mu^{2}),\epsilon\Big)\bigg]\frac{C_{v}}{2}+K_{B_{v}}\Big(\alpha_{s}(\mu^{2}),\epsilon\Big)\Bigg\}\,.

Perturbative expansion

Given the non-commuting nature of the color
operators in eq. (
390
), the expansion
in powers of
\alpha_{s}
of eq. (
389
)
requires the repeated application of the
Zassenhaus formula:

e^{k(X+Y)}=e^{kX}\,e^{kY}\,e^{-\frac{k^{2}}{2}[X,Y]}\,e^{{\cal O}(k^{3})},

(402)

where
X
,
Y
represent two non-commuting
color operators, and
k
a c-number.
In what follows we define the
perturbative expansion of the factors
K_{m}(\alpha_{s})

in eqs. (
392
), (
398
) and (
399
)
as follows:

K_{m}\Big(\alpha_{s}(\mu^{2}),\epsilon\Big)=\sum_{n}\bigg(\frac{\alpha_{s}(\mu^{2})}{\pi}\bigg)^{n}K^{(n)}_{m}\Big(\mu^{2},\epsilon\Big),

(403)

then we have

\displaystyle K^{(1)}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\frac{\gamma_{K}^{(1)}}{4\epsilon},

\displaystyle K^{(2)}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle-\frac{b_{0}\gamma_{K}^{(1)}}{32\epsilon^{2}}+\frac{\gamma_{K}^{(2)}}{8\epsilon},

(404)

\displaystyle K_{D}^{(1)}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle-\frac{\gamma_{K}^{(1)}}{4\epsilon^{2}},

\displaystyle K_{D}^{(2)}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\frac{3b_{0}\gamma_{K}^{(1)}}{64\epsilon^{3}}-\frac{\gamma_{K}^{(2)}}{16\epsilon^{2}},

(405)

\displaystyle K_{B_{q}}^{(1)}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\frac{\gamma_{q}^{(1)}}{2\epsilon},

\displaystyle K_{B_{q}}^{(2)}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle-\frac{b_{0}\gamma_{q}^{(1)}}{16\epsilon^{2}}+\frac{\gamma_{q}^{(2)}}{4\epsilon},

(406)

\displaystyle K_{B_{g}}^{(1)}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\frac{\gamma_{g}^{(1)}}{2\epsilon},

\displaystyle K_{B_{g}}^{(2)}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle-\frac{b_{0}\gamma_{g}^{(1)}}{16\epsilon^{2}}+\frac{\gamma_{g}^{(2)}}{4\epsilon},

(407)

where in turn

\displaystyle\gamma_{K}^{(1)}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle 2,

\displaystyle\gamma_{K}^{(2)}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\bigg(\frac{67}{18}-\frac{\pi^{2}}{6}\bigg)C_{A}-\frac{5}{9}n_{f},

(408)

\displaystyle\gamma_{q}^{(1)}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle-\frac{3}{4}C_{F},

\displaystyle\gamma_{q}^{(2)}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\frac{C_{F}^{2}}{16}\bigg(-\frac{3}{2}+2\pi^{2}-24\zeta_{3}\bigg)+\frac{C_{A}C_{F}}{16}\bigg(-\frac{961}{54}-\frac{11}{6}\pi^{2}26\zeta_{3}\bigg)

(409)

\displaystyle+\,\frac{C_{F}T_{R}n_{f}}{16}\bigg(\frac{130}{27}+\frac{2}{3}\pi^{2}\bigg),

\displaystyle\gamma_{g}^{(1)}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle-\frac{b_{0}}{4},

\displaystyle\gamma_{g}^{(2)}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\frac{C_{A}^{2}}{16}\bigg(-\frac{692}{27}+\frac{11}{18}\pi^{2}+2\zeta_{3}\bigg)+\frac{C_{A}n_{f}}{32}\bigg(\frac{256}{27}-\frac{2}{9}\pi^{2}\bigg)+\frac{C_{F}n_{f}}{8},

(410)

and in turn

b_{0}=\frac{1}{3}\big(11C_{A}-2n_{f}\big),\qquad\qquad b_{1}=\frac{1}{6}\Big[17C_{A}^{2}-\big(10C_{A}+6C_{F}\big)T_{R}n_{f}\Big].

(411)

Then, at tree level and first order
in perturbation theory one has

\displaystyle{\cal M}^{(0)}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle{\cal H}^{(0)},

\displaystyle{\cal M}^{(1)}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\bigg\{K^{(1)}\bigg[\Big(\eta_{1}\mathbf{T}_{t_{1}}^{2}+\eta_{2}\mathbf{T}_{t_{2}}^{2}\Big)+\frac{i\pi}{2}\Big(\mathbf{T}_{(--)}+\mathbf{T}_{(+-)}+\mathbf{T}_{(+-)}\Big)

(412)

\displaystyle\hskip 14.22636pt+\,C_{i}\log\frac{|\mathbf{p}_{5}|^{2}}{\mu^{2}}+\frac{C_{v}}{2}\bigg(\log\frac{|\mathbf{p}_{4}|^{2}}{\mu^{2}}-\frac{i\pi}{2}\bigg)+C_{j}\log\frac{|\mathbf{p}_{3}|^{2}}{\lambda^{2}}\bigg]

\displaystyle\hskip 14.22636pt+\,K_{D}^{(1)}\bigg(C_{i}+\frac{C_{v}}{2}+C_{j}\bigg)+2K_{B_{i}}^{(1)}+K_{B_{v}}^{(1)}+2K_{B_{j}}^{(1)}\bigg\}{\cal H}^{(0)}+{\cal H}^{(1)},

and second order the
factorization formula gives

\displaystyle{\cal M}^{(2)}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\Bigg\{\frac{1}{2}\Big(K^{(1)}\Big)^{2}\Bigg[\eta_{1}^{2}\Big(\mathbf{T}_{t_{1}}^{2}\Big)^{2}+\eta_{1}\eta_{2}\Big\{\mathbf{T}_{t_{1}}^{2},\mathbf{T}_{t_{2}}^{2}\Big\}+\eta_{2}^{2}\Big(\mathbf{T}_{t_{2}}^{2}\Big)^{2}

(413)

\displaystyle+\,\frac{i\pi}{2}\bigg(\eta_{1}\Big\{\mathbf{T}_{(--)}+\mathbf{T}_{(-+)}+\mathbf{T}_{(+-)},\mathbf{T}_{t_{1}}^{2}\Big\}+\eta_{2}\Big\{\mathbf{T}_{(--)}+\mathbf{T}_{(-+)}+\mathbf{T}_{(+-)},\mathbf{T}_{t_{2}}^{2}\Big\}

\displaystyle-\,\frac{\pi^{2}}{4}\Big(\mathbf{T}_{(--)}+\mathbf{T}_{(-+)}+\mathbf{T}_{(+-)}\Big)^{2}\Bigg]+K^{(2)}\Bigg[\eta_{1}\mathbf{T}_{t_{1}}^{2}+\eta_{2}\mathbf{T}_{t_{2}}^{2}

\displaystyle+\,\frac{i\pi}{2}\Big(\mathbf{T}_{(--)}+\mathbf{T}_{(-+)}+\mathbf{T}_{(+-)}\Big)\Bigg]+\frac{C_{i}^{2}}{2}\Bigg[\Big(K_{D}^{(1)}\Big)^{2}+2K_{D}^{(1)}K^{(1)}\log\bigg(\frac{|\mathbf{p}_{5}|^{2}}{\mu^{2}}\bigg)

\displaystyle+\,\Big(K^{(1)}\Big)^{2}\log^{2}\bigg(\frac{|\mathbf{p}_{5}|^{2}}{\mu^{2}}\bigg)\Bigg]+\frac{C_{v}^{2}}{4}\Bigg[\frac{1}{2}\Big(K_{D}^{(1)}\Big)^{2}+K^{(1)}K_{D}^{(1)}\bigg(\log\bigg(\frac{|\mathbf{p}_{4}|^{2}}{\mu^{2}}\bigg)-\frac{i\pi}{2}\bigg)

\displaystyle+\,\frac{1}{2}\Big(K^{(1)}\Big)^{2}\bigg(\log^{2}\bigg(\frac{|\mathbf{p}_{4}|^{2}}{\mu^{2}}\bigg)-i\pi\log\bigg(\frac{|\mathbf{p}_{4}|^{2}}{\mu^{2}}\bigg)-\frac{\pi^{2}}{4}\bigg)\Bigg]

\displaystyle+\,\frac{C_{j}^{2}}{2}\Bigg[\Big(K_{D}^{(1)}\Big)^{2}+2K_{D}^{(1)}K^{(1)}\log\bigg(\frac{|\mathbf{p}_{3}|^{2}}{\mu^{2}}\bigg)+\Big(K^{(1)}\Big)^{2}\log^{2}\bigg(\frac{|\mathbf{p}_{3}|^{2}}{\mu^{2}}\bigg)\Bigg]

\displaystyle+\,C_{i}\Bigg[\bigg(K^{(1)}K_{D}^{(1)}+\Big(K^{(1)}\Big)^{2}\log\bigg(\frac{|\mathbf{p}_{5}|^{2}}{\mu^{2}}\bigg)\bigg)\bigg(\eta_{1}\mathbf{T}_{t1}^{2}+\eta_{2}\mathbf{T}_{t_{2}}^{2}

\displaystyle+\,\frac{i\pi}{2}\Big(\mathbf{T}_{(--)}+\mathbf{T}_{(-+)}+\mathbf{T}_{(+-)}\Big)\bigg)+K^{(2)}\log\bigg(\frac{|\mathbf{p}_{5}|^{2}}{\mu^{2}}\bigg)

\displaystyle+\,\bigg(K^{(1)}\log\bigg(\frac{|\mathbf{p}_{5}|^{2}}{\mu^{2}}\bigg)+K_{D}^{(1)}\bigg)\Big(2B_{i}^{(1)}+2B_{j}^{(1)}+B_{v}^{(1)}\Big)+K_{D}^{(2)}

\displaystyle+\,\frac{C_{v}}{2}\bigg[\Big(K_{D}^{(1)}\Big)^{2}+K^{(1)}K_{D}^{(1)}\bigg(\log\bigg(\frac{|\mathbf{p}_{4}|^{2}}{\mu^{2}}\bigg)+\log\bigg(\frac{|\mathbf{p}_{5}|^{2}}{\mu^{2}}\bigg)-\frac{i\pi}{2}\bigg)

\displaystyle+\,\Big(K^{(1)}\Big)^{2}\log\bigg(\frac{|\mathbf{p}_{5}|^{2}}{\mu^{2}}\bigg)\bigg(\log\bigg(\frac{|\mathbf{p}_{4}|^{2}}{\mu^{2}}\bigg)-\frac{i\pi}{2}\bigg)\bigg]\Bigg]

\displaystyle+\,C_{v}\Bigg[\frac{1}{2}\Big(K^{(1)}\Big)^{2}\bigg(\Big(\eta_{1}\mathbf{T}_{t1}^{2}+\eta_{2}\mathbf{T}_{t_{2}}^{2}\Big)\log\bigg(\frac{|\mathbf{p}_{4}|^{2}}{\mu^{2}}\bigg)-\frac{i\pi}{2}\bigg(\eta_{1}\mathbf{T}_{t_{1}}^{2}+\eta_{2}\mathbf{T}_{t_{2}}^{2}

\displaystyle-\,\big(\mathbf{T}_{(--)}+\mathbf{T}_{(-+)}+\mathbf{T}_{(+-)}\Big)\log\bigg(\frac{|\mathbf{p}_{4}|^{2}}{\mu^{2}}\bigg)\bigg)+\frac{\pi^{2}}{4}\Big(\mathbf{T}_{(--)}+\mathbf{T}_{(-+)}+\mathbf{T}_{(+-)}\Big)\bigg)

\displaystyle+\,K^{(1)}\bigg(\frac{1}{2}K_{D}^{(1)}\Big(\eta_{1}\mathbf{T}_{t_{1}}^{2}+\eta_{2}\mathbf{T}_{t_{2}}^{2}\Big)-\frac{i\pi}{2}\bigg(B_{i}^{(1)}+B_{j}^{(1)}+\frac{1}{2}B_{v}^{(1)}

\displaystyle-\,\frac{1}{2}K_{D}^{(1)}\Big(\mathbf{T}_{(--)}+\mathbf{T}_{(-+)}+\mathbf{T}_{(+-)}\Big)\bigg)+\bigg(B_{i}^{(1)}+B_{j}^{(1)}+\frac{1}{2}B_{v}^{(1)}\bigg)\log\bigg(\frac{|\mathbf{p}_{4}|^{2}}{\mu^{2}}\bigg)

\displaystyle+\,\frac{K^{(2)}}{2}\bigg(\log\bigg(\frac{|\mathbf{p}_{4}|^{2}}{\mu^{2}}\bigg)-\frac{i\pi}{2}\bigg)+K_{D}^{(1)}\Big(B_{i}^{(1)}+B_{j}^{(1)}+\frac{1}{2}B_{v}^{(1)}\Big)+\frac{1}{2}K_{D}^{(2)}\bigg)\Bigg]

\displaystyle+\,C_{j}\Bigg[\bigg(K^{(1)}K_{D}^{(1)}+\Big(K^{(1)}\Big)^{2}\log\bigg(\frac{|\mathbf{p}_{3}|^{2}}{\mu^{2}}\bigg)\bigg(\eta_{1}\mathbf{T}_{t_{1}}^{2}+\eta_{2}\mathbf{T}_{t_{2}}^{2}

\displaystyle+\,\frac{i\pi}{2}\Big(\mathbf{T}_{(--)}+\mathbf{T}_{(-+)}+\mathbf{T}_{(+-)}\Big)\bigg)+K^{(2)}\log\bigg(\frac{|\mathbf{p}_{3}|^{2}}{\mu^{2}}\bigg)

\displaystyle+\,\bigg(K^{(1)}\log\bigg(\frac{|\mathbf{p}_{3}|^{2}}{\mu^{2}}\bigg)+K_{D}^{(1)}\bigg)\Big(2B_{i}^{(1)}+2B_{j}^{(1)}+B_{v}^{(1)}\Big)+K_{D}^{(2)}

\displaystyle+\,\frac{C_{v}}{2}\bigg[\Big(K_{D}^{(1)}\Big)^{2}+K^{(1)}K_{D}^{(1)}\bigg(\log\bigg(\frac{|\mathbf{p}_{3}|^{2}}{\mu^{2}}\bigg)+\log\bigg(\frac{|\mathbf{p}_{4}|^{2}}{\mu^{2}}\bigg)-\frac{i\pi}{2}\bigg)

\displaystyle+\,\Big(K^{(1)}\Big)^{2}\log\bigg(\frac{|\mathbf{p}_{3}|^{2}}{\mu^{2}}\bigg)\bigg(\log\bigg(\frac{|\mathbf{p}_{4}|^{2}}{\mu^{2}}\bigg)-\frac{i\pi}{2}\bigg)\bigg]\Bigg]

\displaystyle+\,C_{i}C_{j}\Bigg[\Big(K_{D}^{(1)}\Big)^{2}+K_{D}^{(1)}K^{(1)}\bigg(\log\bigg(\frac{|\mathbf{p}_{3}|^{2}}{\mu^{2}}\bigg)+\log\bigg(\frac{|\mathbf{p}_{5}|^{2}}{\mu^{2}}\bigg)\bigg)

\displaystyle+\,2B_{i}^{(2)}+2B_{j}^{(2)}+2B_{i}^{(1)}B_{v}^{(1)}+2B_{j}^{(1)}B_{v}^{(1)}+\frac{1}{2}\Big(B_{v}^{(1)}\Big)^{2}+B_{v}^{(2)}

\displaystyle+\,K^{(1)}\bigg(\eta_{1}\mathbf{T}_{t_{1}}^{2}+\eta_{2}\mathbf{T}_{t_{2}}^{2}+\frac{i\pi}{2}\Big(\mathbf{T}_{(--)}+\mathbf{T}_{(-+)}+\mathbf{T}_{(+-)}\Big)\bigg)

\displaystyle\times\,\Big(2B_{i}^{(1)}+2B_{j}^{(1)}+B_{v}^{(1)}\Big)\Bigg]\Bigg\}{\cal H}^{(0)}

\displaystyle+\,\bigg\{K^{(1)}\bigg[\Big(\eta_{1}\mathbf{T}_{t_{1}}^{2}+\eta_{2}\mathbf{T}_{t_{2}}^{2}\Big)+\frac{i\pi}{2}\Big(\mathbf{T}_{(--)}+\mathbf{T}_{(-+)}+\mathbf{T}_{(+-)}\Big)\bigg]

\displaystyle+\,C_{i}\bigg[K_{D}^{(1)}+K^{(1)}\log\frac{|\mathbf{p}_{5}|^{2}}{\mu^{2}}\bigg]+\frac{C_{v}}{2}\bigg[K_{D}^{(1)}+K^{(1)}\bigg(\log\frac{|\mathbf{p}_{4}|^{2}}{\mu^{2}}-\frac{i\pi}{2}\bigg)\bigg]

\displaystyle\hskip 14.22636pt+\,C_{j}\bigg[K_{D}^{(1)}+K^{(1)}\log\frac{|\mathbf{p}_{3}|^{2}}{\lambda^{2}}\bigg]+2K_{B_{i}}^{(1)}+K_{B_{v}}^{(1)}+2K_{B_{j}}^{(1)}\bigg\}{\cal H}^{(1)}+{\cal H}^{(2)},

where we have defined

\big\{{\bf\cal O}_{1},{\bf\cal O}_{2}\big\}={\bf\cal O}_{1}{\bf\cal O}_{2}+{\bf\cal O}_{2}{\bf\cal O}_{1},

(414)

with
{\bf\cal O}_{1}
,
{\bf\cal O}_{2}

two non-commuting color operators.

To conclude this section, let us recall that
in section
7.3
we have evaluated
only the odd-odd amplitude at two loops, thus
we need to extract the
{\cal M}^{(-,-)}

component from eq. (
413
).
In general, at all orders in perturbation
theory this is obtained expanding systematically

{\bf Z}
and
{\cal H}
into their
(\pm,pm)

components. Then one has

{\cal M}^{(-,-)}={\bf Z}^{(-,-)}{\cal H}^{(+,+)}+{\bf Z}^{(-,+)}{\cal H}^{(+,-)}+{\bf Z}^{(+,-)}{\cal H}^{(-,+)}+{\bf Z}^{(+,+)}{\cal H}^{(-,-)}.

(415)

In practice, at two loops we have

\displaystyle{\cal M}^{(-,-,2)}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\Bigg\{\frac{1}{2}\Big(K^{(1)}\Big)^{2}\Bigg[\eta_{1}^{2}\Big(\mathbf{T}_{t_{1}}^{2}\Big)^{2}+\eta_{1}\eta_{2}\Big\{\mathbf{T}_{t_{1}}^{2},\mathbf{T}_{t_{2}}^{2}\Big\}+\eta_{2}^{2}\Big(\mathbf{T}_{t_{2}}^{2}\Big)^{2}

(416)

\displaystyle-\,\frac{\pi^{2}}{4}\Big(\mathbf{T}^{2}_{(--)}+\mathbf{T}^{2}_{(-+)}+\mathbf{T}^{2}_{(+-)}\Big)\Bigg]+K^{(2)}\Big[\eta_{1}\mathbf{T}_{t_{1}}^{2}+\eta_{2}\mathbf{T}_{t_{2}}^{2}\Big]

\displaystyle+\,\frac{C_{i}^{2}}{2}\Bigg[\Big(K_{D}^{(1)}\Big)^{2}+2K_{D}^{(1)}K^{(1)}\log\bigg(\frac{|\mathbf{p}_{5}|^{2}}{\mu^{2}}\bigg)+\Big(K^{(1)}\Big)^{2}\log^{2}\bigg(\frac{|\mathbf{p}_{5}|^{2}}{\mu^{2}}\bigg)\Bigg]

\displaystyle+\,\frac{C_{v}^{2}}{4}\Bigg[\frac{1}{2}\Big(K_{D}^{(1)}\Big)^{2}+K^{(1)}K_{D}^{(1)}\bigg(\log\bigg(\frac{|\mathbf{p}_{4}|^{2}}{\mu^{2}}\bigg)-\frac{i\pi}{2}\bigg)

\displaystyle+\,\frac{C_{j}^{2}}{2}\Bigg[\Big(K_{D}^{(1)}\Big)^{2}+2K_{D}^{(1)}K^{(1)}\log\bigg(\frac{|\mathbf{p}_{3}|^{2}}{\mu^{2}}\bigg)+\Big(K^{(1)}\Big)^{2}\log^{2}\bigg(\frac{|\mathbf{p}_{3}|^{2}}{\mu^{2}}\bigg)\Bigg]

\displaystyle+\,C_{i}\Bigg[\bigg(K^{(1)}K_{D}^{(1)}+\Big(K^{(1)}\Big)^{2}\log\bigg(\frac{|\mathbf{p}_{5}|^{2}}{\mu^{2}}\bigg)\bigg)\bigg(\eta_{1}\mathbf{T}_{t1}^{2}+\eta_{2}\mathbf{T}_{t_{2}}^{2}\bigg)

\displaystyle+K^{(2)}\log\bigg(\frac{|\mathbf{p}_{5}|^{2}}{\mu^{2}}\bigg)+\bigg(K^{(1)}\log\bigg(\frac{|\mathbf{p}_{5}|^{2}}{\mu^{2}}\bigg)+K_{D}^{(1)}\bigg)\Big(2B_{i}^{(1)}+2B_{j}^{(1)}+B_{v}^{(1)}\Big)

\displaystyle+\,K_{D}^{(2)}+\frac{C_{v}}{2}\bigg[\Big(K_{D}^{(1)}\Big)^{2}+K^{(1)}K_{D}^{(1)}\bigg(\log\bigg(\frac{|\mathbf{p}_{4}|^{2}}{\mu^{2}}\bigg)+\log\bigg(\frac{|\mathbf{p}_{5}|^{2}}{\mu^{2}}\bigg)-\frac{i\pi}{2}\bigg)

\displaystyle+\,\Big(K^{(1)}\Big)^{2}\log\bigg(\frac{|\mathbf{p}_{5}|^{2}}{\mu^{2}}\bigg)\bigg(\log\bigg(\frac{|\mathbf{p}_{4}|^{2}}{\mu^{2}}\bigg)-\frac{i\pi}{2}\bigg)\bigg]\Bigg]

\displaystyle+\,C_{v}\Bigg[\frac{1}{2}\Big(K^{(1)}\Big)^{2}\Big(\eta_{1}\mathbf{T}_{t1}^{2}+\eta_{2}\mathbf{T}_{t_{2}}^{2}\Big)\bigg(\log\bigg(\frac{|\mathbf{p}_{4}|^{2}}{\mu^{2}}\bigg)-\frac{i\pi}{2}\bigg)

\displaystyle+\,K^{(1)}\bigg(\frac{1}{2}K_{D}^{(1)}\Big(\eta_{1}\mathbf{T}_{t_{1}}^{2}+\eta_{2}\mathbf{T}_{t_{2}}^{2}\Big)-\frac{i\pi}{2}\bigg(B_{i}^{(1)}+B_{j}^{(1)}+\frac{1}{2}B_{v}^{(1)}\bigg)

\displaystyle+\,C_{j}\Bigg[\bigg(K^{(1)}K_{D}^{(1)}+\Big(K^{(1)}\Big)^{2}\log\bigg(\frac{|\mathbf{p}_{3}|^{2}}{\mu^{2}}\bigg)\bigg(\eta_{1}\mathbf{T}_{t_{1}}^{2}+\eta_{2}\mathbf{T}_{t_{2}}^{2}\bigg)

\displaystyle+\,K^{(2)}\log\bigg(\frac{|\mathbf{p}_{3}|^{2}}{\mu^{2}}\bigg)+\bigg(K^{(1)}\log\bigg(\frac{|\mathbf{p}_{3}|^{2}}{\mu^{2}}\bigg)+K_{D}^{(1)}\bigg)\Big(2B_{i}^{(1)}+2B_{j}^{(1)}+B_{v}^{(1)}\Big)

\displaystyle+\,\bigg\{K^{(1)}\bigg[\Big(\eta_{1}\mathbf{T}_{t_{1}}^{2}+\eta_{2}\mathbf{T}_{t_{2}}^{2}\Big)\bigg]+C_{i}\bigg[K_{D}^{(1)}+K^{(1)}\log\frac{|\mathbf{p}_{5}|^{2}}{\mu^{2}}\bigg]

\displaystyle\hskip 14.22636pt+\,2K_{B_{i}}^{(1)}+K_{B_{v}}^{(1)}+2K_{B_{j}}^{(1)}\bigg\}{\cal H}^{(-,-,1)}+K^{(1)}\frac{i\pi}{2}\Big[\mathbf{T}_{(+-)}{\cal H}^{(-,+,1)}

\displaystyle+\,\mathbf{T}_{(-+)}{\cal H}^{(+,-,1)}+\mathbf{T}_{(--)}{\cal H}^{(+,+,1)}\Big]+{\cal H}^{(-,-,2)}.
